# What Are the Different Types of TMT Steel Bars Used in Construction

### What Are the Different Types of TMT Steel Bars Used in Construction

![Calender](https://d3vvmt2ub6odwk.cloudfront.net/static/pantherv2/dist/images/celender.png) 2024-04-06 ![Calender Icon](https://d3vvmt2ub6odwk.cloudfront.net/static/pantherv2/dist/images/team.png) Team Jindal Panther

Different Types of [TMT Steel Bars](https://www.jindalpanther.com/tmt-rebar.html): Also referred to as Thermo Mechanically Treated bars, made their debut in the construction industry in 1979. Crafted from high-grade steel, these bars boast exceptional strength, significantly enhancing the structural stability of buildings. The market offers a spectrum of steel bar types, differing in steel grade and thickness, with the commonly utilized grades being Fe 415, Fe 500, Fe 550, and Fe 550D. The robustness and endurance of each TMT bar depend on the steel grade used and the chemical composition encompassing Carbon, Sulphur, and Phosphorous.  
  
In India, TMT steel bars are available in various dimensions, spanning from 8mm to 10mm, 12mm, 16mm, 18mm, 20mm, and 32mm. Hence, customizing [TMT Bars](https://www.jindalpanther.com/tmt-rebar) to specific requirements is also feasible for those seeking customized sizes. In order to do this, it is essential to start by getting familiar with the different types of steel bars out there, what they're used for, and the pros and cons of each.

## **What are TMT Steel Bars?**

TMT steel bars, or Thermo-Mechanically Treated steel bars, are high-strength reinforcement bars with a tough outer core and a soft inner core. They are used in construction for enhanced protection against earthquakes and other natural disasters. Primarily, TMT steel bars, like the Jindal Panther [TMT Bars](https://www.jindalpanther.com/tmt-rebar) (Experienced TMT rebar distributor), stand out for their exceptional quality, offering unmatched strength, safety, ductility, and flexibility that meet modern construction demands. Made from high-quality mild steel and processed through advanced HYQST (High Yield Quenching and Self Tempering) technology, these bars, available in grades like Fe 500D and Fe 550D, promise superior strength, durability, and anti-rust properties.

Their widespread use in construction owes to their robust material strength, impeccable finish, and resistance to rust. Thanks to their low carbon content, these bars allow safer and quicker welding without the need for preheating. Crafted meticulously using virgin iron ore and precise chemical controls, Jindal Panther [TMT Bars](https://www.jindalpanther.com/tmt-rebar) ensure outstanding performance.  
  
**Note** - What's more, Jindal Panther (Experienced TMT rebar distributor) follows a fair and transparent pricing policy for consistency and is backed by an extensive dealer network, guaranteeing exceptional service throughout India.

### **Different Types of TMT Steel Bars Used in Construction**

In construction, a range of steel bars exists, differing in types, grades, and their unique properties. Here are the primary types and grades of TMT steel bars Listed below:

![](https://docs.jindalpanther.com/jindalpanther/post/1717816134TypesofTMTSteelBarswithJindalPanther.webp)

### **Types of TMT Steel Bars**

There are different kinds of steel bars used in building and construction projects. They have names like Fe 415, Fe 500, Fe 550, and Fe 600. Each type does a different job. These bars are made from really strong steel and help make buildings sturdy. Some are good for small buildings, others for big ones. They're like puzzle pieces, fitting into projects perfectly to make them strong and safe. Here is the list of prime types listed below:

#### **1. Mild Steel Plain Bars**

Mild steel bars are completely flat and lack raised ridges, typically ranging from 6mm to 50mm in diameter. They find common usage in concrete construction. Crafted from low-carbon steel, these bars are adaptable to various processing methods like machining, jigs, and templates. Their attributes include good strength, welding capabilities, and moderate machining properties. These bars are often used in bracing, ductwork, overhead doors, and heating systems. The flat bar, also known as a rectangular bar, comes in either cold-drawn material with a smooth finish or keystock with precise tolerances.

| Sr. No. | Property | Mild Steel Bars |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | Description | Completely flat and smooth bars |
| 2 | Diameter Range | 6mm to 50mm |
| 3 | Material | Low carbon steel |
| 4 | Common Uses | Concrete construction,  Bracing,  Ductwork |
| 5 | Processing | Machining,  Jigs,  Templates |
| 6 | Characteristics | Good strength, welding capability |

#### **2. Hot Rolled Deformed Bars**

Hot Rolled Deformed Bars, also known as Thermo Mechanically Treated (TMT) bars, undergo a hot rolling process resulting in a ribbed structure, offering enhanced concrete grip. Their tensile yield strength surpasses other steel reinforcement bars (i.e. [Stirrups](https://www.jindalpanther.com/stirrups.html), [Footings](https://www.jindalpanther.com/footings.html)), making them pivotal in larger construction projects requiring substantial steel. These bars excel in reinforcing concrete structures, compensating for concrete's limited tensile strength.

| Sr. No. | Property | Hot Rolled Deformed Bars (TMT bars) |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | Production Process | Hot rolling process and [binding wire](https://www.jindalpanther.com/binding-wire.html) |
| 2 | Structure | Ribbed surface for enhanced concrete grip |
| 3 | Strength | Higher tensile yield strength compared to other steel reinforcement bars |
| 4 | Common Uses | Reinforcing large construction projects (Stirrups, Footings), Reinforced concrete structures |
| 5 | Characteristics | Surface deformations for better concrete bond, Increased ductility |

#### **3. Cold Worked Steel Bars**

Cold Worked Steel Bars undergo a cold working procedure aimed at bolstering their strength. This method involves pulling the steel bar through a smaller die compared to its initial hot-rolled size. This process enhances the bar's dimensional tolerances, straightness, surface appearance, and overall physical properties. Cold-worked steel bars, due to their capacity to be bent into intricate shapes, find frequent usage in construction projects that demand higher steel requirements.

| Sr. No. | Property | Cold Worked Steel Bars |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | Production Process | The cold working method, Pulling through a smaller die |
| 2 | Purpose | Enhances strength and properties, Improves dimensional accuracy |
| 3 | Types | Cold-finished steel bars: Turned bars or Cold-drawn bars |
| 4 | Characteristics | Improved dimensional tolerances, Straightness, and surface finish |
| 5 | Common Uses | Construction projects requiring higher steel requirements |

**Note:** Compared to hot-rolled steel bars, cold-worked steel bars boast tighter size and roundness tolerances, precise straightness, and a smoother surface finish.

#### **4. Twisted Steel Bars:**

Twisted steel bars undergo shaping by twisting to elevate their strength and stability. Their versatile nature allows for varied applications in construction. Key attributes of twisted steel bars include:

* **Increased Strength and Stability:** The twisting process fortifies the steel bar, enhancing its resilience against wear and tear.
* **Versatility:** These bars find utility in diverse construction areas—handrails, gates, grills, balusters, pickets, and various ironwork projects.
* **Decorative Applications:** Twisted steel bars facilitate the creation of intricate designs like birdcage or split twist patterns. These ornate designs are often incorporated into architectural elements like balusters and newel posts.
* **Bonding with Concrete:** Used as reinforcement bars, twisted steel bars augment concrete structures, bestowing added tensile strength and flexibility to the concrete.

#### **5. Welded Steel Bars**

Welded steel bars are made by joining steel sheets through welding and can be adjusted to fit specific project needs. They're commonly seen in reinforced concrete construction and used in buildings, bridges, highways, locks, dams, docks, and piers.  
  
In construction nowadays, welding is becoming more important for joining concrete reinforcing bars. Three common welding methods: shielded metal arc welding, gas metal arc welding, and thermit welding are used for this. These methods help create strong connections between the bars, making concrete structures stronger and steadier. There's also a type of rebar called weldable rebar, made from low-alloy mild steel. It's great for welding because it has a low steel-to-carbon ratio.

| Sr. No. | Property | Welded Steel Bars | Weldable Rebar |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | Production Process | Formed by the process of welding steel sheets | Low alloy mild steel rebar, weld-friendly |
| 2 | Customization | Customizable based on project requirements | Suitable for welding due to low steel-to-carbon ratio |
| 3 | Common Use | Reinforced concrete construction | Used in construction, remains intact under heavy loads |
| 4 | Applications | Buildings, Bridges, Highways, Dams, Docks, etc. | Flexible, Easy to bend and weld, Suitable for various jobs |
| 5 | Importance in Welding | Used for splicing concrete reinforcing bars | Offers strength and stability to concrete structures |

### 

### **Grades of TMT Steel Bars**

TMT steel bars are categorized based on their yield stress, and there are common grades like Fe 415, Fe 500, Fe 550, and Fe 600. 

* Fe 415 steel bars are known for being economical and flexible, making them ideal for small-scale construction projects and reinforcing residential homes.
* On the other hand, Fe 500 bars are designed specifically for industrial use, finding their place in large-format infrastructure projects like bridges and commercial buildings.
* Fe 550 bars strike a balance between strength and ductility, catering to a variety of construction needs due to their versatile nature.
* Meanwhile, Fe 600 bars offer exceptional strength and ductility, making them a top choice for critical structures and projects that demand superior performance.

| Sr. No. | Grade | Description | Applications |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | Fe 415 | Economical and flexible | Small-scale construction, Residential reinforcement |
| 2 | Fe 500 | Specifically for industrial use | Bridges, Commercial buildings, Large infrastructure |
| 3 | Fe 550 | Balances strength and ductility | Various construction needs |
| 4 | Fe 600 | Offers high strength and ductility | Critical structures, Superior performance projects |

## **Conclusion**

In the dominance of construction, TMT steel bars stand tall as the unsung heroes, weaving strength and reliability into the very fabric of buildings. Fe 415, Fe 500, Fe 550, and Fe 600. These aren't just names but symbols of resilience, each designed with a specific purpose. These bars, born from the fusion of high-grade steel and cutting-edge technology, promise not just strength but a shield against nature's might.

## **What Are the Different Types of TMT Steel Bars FAQs**

**Q. What is a TMT steel bar?**

**Ans:** TMT stands for Thermo-Mechanically Treated steel bars, known for their enhanced strength and flexibility due to a specialized manufacturing process.

**Q. How is the TMT steel bar graded?**

**Ans:** TMT bars are graded based on their yield stress, commonly categorized as Fe 415, Fe 500, Fe 550, and Fe 600, each with distinct properties.

**Q. What is the difference between Fe 415 and Fe 500 TMT bars?**

**Ans:** Fe 415 bars are cost-effective and ductile, suitable for small-scale projects. Fe 500 bars, stronger, are used in larger industrial and infrastructure projects.

**Q. Which TMT bar is best for high-stress structures?**

**Ans:** Fe 600 TMT bars offer superior strength and ductility, making them ideal for critical structures requiring top-notch performance.

**Q. Is TMT steel bar suitable for residential construction?**

**Ans:** Yes, particularly Fe 415 bars, known for their flexibility and cost-effectiveness, making them suitable for reinforcing residential homes.

# What Is a TMT Bar? Meaning, Manufacturing, Grades, Uses & Benefits

# What Is a TMT Bar? Meaning, Manufacturing, Grades, Uses & Benefits

![Calender](https://d3vvmt2ub6odwk.cloudfront.net/static/pantherv2/dist/images/celender.png)
2026-22-01 ![Calender Icon](https://d3vvmt2ub6odwk.cloudfront.net/static/pantherv2/dist/images/team.png)
Team Jindal Panther

  


When construction work starts, the
strength and safety of a building mainly depend on the TMT bar. Although it
stays hidden inside the concrete, it carries the weight of the structure for
many years. That is why it is important to understand what a TMT bar is
before starting any residential or commercial construction.

TMT bars are designed to handle
stress without breaking. This makes them suitable for homes, high-rise
buildings, and infrastructure projects.

## **What Is a TMT Bar?**

A **TMT Bar** is a
steel reinforcement bar used inside concrete to make buildings strong and
stable. **Concrete** is strong under pressure but weak when
pulled, and TMT bars help solve this problem by providing tensile strength.
TMT bars have become the preferred reinforcement for modern RCC construction
due to their superior performance.

In simple words, when people ask
**what is a TMT bar,** they are asking about the steel that
protects a building from cracks, collapse, and long-term structural
damage.

### **Key Features of a TMT Bar**

* Used to reinforce RCC structures
* Strong outer surface with a flexible inner core
* Handles load, pressure, and movement
* Suitable for houses, buildings, and infrastructure

## **Meaning of TMT in Steel**

The meaning of **TMT in
steel** comes from the way the steel is processed during
manufacturing. TMT stands for **Thermo-Mechanically Treated,**
which means the steel goes through heat and controlled cooling to improve
its performance. In construction, **TMT bar and TMT rebar are the same
and are simply different names used for this treated steel.**

This process changes the internal
structure of the steel. The outer layer becomes hard and strong, while the
inner core remains soft and flexible. Because of this balance, **TMT
steel** performs much better than normal steel inside concrete.

### **What TMT in Steel Improves**

* Higher strength and load-bearing capacity
* Better flexibility and bending ability
* Reduced risk of sudden breakage
* Strong bonding with concrete

## **How TMT Bars Are Manufactured**

The manufacturing process of
**TMT bars** is the main reason behind their quality. These
bars are not just shaped steel rods, they are carefully treated to achieve
strength and flexibility together.

![These are not
					numbers for a spreadsheet. They are the hidden grammar of your
					building](https://docs.jindalpanther.com/document/123/1769060272_b38b2228-f96b-4fac-8e44-d227273596cf.jpg)

First, steel bars are heated at
very high temperatures. Then, they are rapidly cooled using water jets,
which hardens the outer surface. After this, the inner core cools slowly and
stays flexible.

### **TMT Bar Manufacturing Process**

* Heating steel to high temperature
* Rapid surface cooling using water
* Formation of hard outer layer
* Flexible inner core for stress absorption

## **Why TMT Bars Are Better Than Normal Steel**

Earlier, mild **steel was
commonly used in construction,** but it was rigid and often caused
cracks due to temperature changes and ground movement. **TMT bars are
used in construction** because they can bend slightly under
pressure, helping the structure stay stable and reduce cracking over
time.

### **TMT Bar vs Mild Steel Comparison**

| Feature | TMT Bar | Mild Steel |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Strength | High | Moderate |
| Flexibility | High | Low |
| Earthquake resistance | Yes | No |
| Corrosion resistance | Better | Poor |
| Construction life | Long | Short |

## **Strength and Flexibility of TMT Bars**

One of the biggest advantages of
**TMT bars** is the combination of strength and flexibility.
The outer surface carries heavy loads, while the inner core absorbs stress
without breaking.

This balance helps reduce cracks,
minimize damage, and plays an important role in **TMT bars in building
safety** for long-lasting structures.

## **Earthquake Resistance of TMT Bars**

During an earthquake, buildings
shake and move suddenly. This puts a lot of pressure on walls, beams, and
columns. **Earthquake resistant TMT bars** can bend a little
instead of breaking, which helps the building stay strong and safe during
the shaking.

These bars also help the structure
adjust to the movement and prevent big cracks. They keep important parts
like beams and columns strong so the building can survive without serious
damage.

![These are not
					numbers for a spreadsheet. They are the hidden grammar of your
					building](https://docs.jindalpanther.com/document/123/1769060278_309795ca-8d0c-436e-872a-5cbc8391b987.jpg)

**How these TMT bars help in
an earthquake**

* Absorb the shaking from the ground
* Reduce cracks in concrete
* Keep walls, beams, and columns strong
* Make the building safer during earthquakes

That is why earthquake resistant
TMT bars are very important in areas where earthquakes are common.

## **Corrosion Resistance of TMT Steel**

Rust slowly damages steel inside
concrete, which can make walls, beams, and columns weak over time.
**Corrosion resistant TMT bars** are made to prevent this
problem, especially in areas with heavy rain, humidity, or salty coastal
air.

During manufacturing, these bars go
through a special heating and cooling process. The outer layer becomes tough
to resist rust, while the inner core remains flexible for strength. This
makes the steel last longer and keeps the concrete safe from cracks caused
by rust. Using corrosion resistant TMT bars helps ensure that the building
stays strong, stable, and durable for many years.

## **Fire Resistance of TMT Bars**

In a fire, steel inside a building
heats up and starts to expand. Poor-quality steel loses strength quickly and
can cause slabs, beams, or columns to fail early. **Fire resistant TMT
bars** are made to handle high temperatures for a longer time, which
helps the structure stay stable even during a fire.

These bars are manufactured with a
special process that gives them strength and flexibility at high heat.
Because of this, the building gets extra time for evacuation, and the risk
of sudden structural damage is reduced. Using fire resistant TMT bars helps
keep both people and property safer in case of fire.

## **TMT Bar Grades and Their Uses**

TMT bars come in different grades
that show how strong they are and how much load they can handle. Choosing
the right grade is very important because using a weaker bar can make the
building unsafe, while using a stronger bar than needed can increase
construction costs.

Each grade is suitable for
different types of structures. For example, smaller grades may be enough for
houses, while higher grades are better for bridges, commercial buildings,
and industrial structures. Picking the correct grade ensures the building is
safe, durable, and cost-effective.

### **Detailed Table – TMT Bar Grades and Applications**

| TMT Bar Grade | Yield Strength | Best Used For | Why This Grade Is Used |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Fe 415 | 415 N/mm² | Small houses, low-rise buildings | Good flexibility, suitable for light load |
| Fe 500 | 500 N/mm² | Houses, apartments, commercial buildings | Best balance of strength and flexibility |
| Fe 500D | 500 N/mm² (high ductility) | Earthquake-prone areas | Extra flexibility for seismic safety |
| Fe 550 | 550 N/mm² | High-rise buildings | Higher strength, less steel usage |
| Fe 600 | 600 N/mm² | Bridges, flyovers, infrastructure | Very high load-bearing capacity |

## **Where TMT Bars Are Used**

**TMT bars are widely used
across different types of reinforced concrete structures. They are commonly
found in:**

* Independent houses
* Apartments and societies
* Offices and malls
* Bridges and flyovers
* Industrial buildings

Wherever concrete needs internal
strength, TMT bars are essential.

## **How to Choose the Right TMT Bar**

Choosing the right **TMT
bar** is a long-term decision because steel cannot be replaced after
construction. Price should never be the only deciding factor.

Along with checking quality and
grade, buying from [authorised sources](https://www.jindalpanther.com/dealerdistributor-locator) helps ensure original material and
proper pricing.

### **What to Check Before Buying TMT Bars**

* Correct grade as per structure
* Manufacturing quality
* Consistent rib pattern
* Compliance with standards

Good quality TMT bars reduce repair
costs and support [better cost planning](https://www.jindalpanther.com/recommended-consumer-price) for construction projects.

## **Why TMT Bars Matter for Long-Term Safety**

Paint, tiles, and fittings can be
changed later, but steel inside concrete cannot. Poor-quality steel may show
problems after a few years in the form of cracks and weakness.

Using good **TMT
bars** ensures durability, safety, and peace of mind for
decades.

## **FAQs About TMT Bars**

### **Q. What does TMT stand for?**

**Ans:** TMT
stands for Thermo-Mechanically Treated steel. It refers to the heat and
cooling process used to improve steel strength and flexibility.

### **Q. Which TMT bar grade is best for house construction?**

**Ans:** Fe
500 is commonly used for houses because it offers a good balance of strength
and flexibility.

### **Q. Are TMT bars rust-proof?**

**Ans:** TMT
bars are corrosion-resistant, not completely rust-proof. Proper concrete
cover helps prevent rust.

### **Q. Are TMT bars safe for earthquake zones?**

**Ans:** Yes,
TMT bars absorb seismic movement and reduce the risk of sudden collapse.

### **Q. Are TMT bars expensive?**

**Ans:**  They
may cost slightly more per kg, but they save money in long-term repairs and
maintenance.

## **Final Conclusion**

TMT bars are the backbone of modern
construction, providing the strength, flexibility, and durability that
buildings need. Using [Jindal Panther TMT bars](https://www.jindalpanther.com/tmt-rebar)  helps ensure long-term
structural safety and reliable performance.

Choosing quality steel at the
construction stage reduces future risks and costly repairs. This approach
protects the building and supports its strength for many years.

# Woven Mesh Vs Welded Wire Mesh: Which Suits Your Process

### Woven Mesh Vs Welded Wire Mesh: Which Suits Your Process

![Calender](https://d3vvmt2ub6odwk.cloudfront.net/static/pantherv2/dist/images/celender.png) 2024-28-06 ![Calender Icon](https://d3vvmt2ub6odwk.cloudfront.net/static/pantherv2/dist/images/team.png) Team Jindal Panther

[Welded Wire Mesh](https://www.jindalpanther.com/welded-wiremesh.html): Wire mesh is a flexible material useful in various applications, like filtering and fencing. Yet, choosing between woven mesh and welded mesh can pose a challenge in deciding what suits your needs better. Woven wire mesh forms through interlocking [binding wires](https://www.jindalpanther.com/binding-wire.html) during weaving, perfect for tasks like analyzing particles and screen printing.

Meanwhile, welded wire mesh results from welding wires at their intersections, ideal for durable mesh requirements. Each mesh type has its pros and cons, and the choice depends on your specific needs.

  


## **Woven Mesh Vs Welded Wire Mesh - Introduction**

Woven mesh and welded wire mesh are two kinds of wire mesh used for different purposes. Woven mesh is created by weaving wires over and under each other, while welded mesh is made by melting wire intersections together. Also, woven mesh usually uses thinner binding wires, whereas welded mesh uses thicker wires because of the welding process.

Moreover, welded mesh is tougher and sturdier due to its fused bonds, whereas woven mesh is more flexible and maintains its shape better. Primarily, woven mesh tends to be cheaper because it needs less labor to make, while welded mesh is more expensive due to its more involved production. Also, woven mesh suits tasks like filtration and screen printing, while welded mesh is better for heavy-duty uses like fencing and concrete mesh sheets. To choose the right mesh, consider what level of strength, flexibility, and durability your project needs.

| Sr. No. | Aspect | Woven Mesh | Welded Mesh |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | Construction | Crossed over and under wires | Wire intersections fused through heat |
| 2 | Material | Thinner wires | Thicker wires |
| 3 | Durability | More flexible, longer coil memory | Rigid, stronger due to fused bonds |
| 4 | Cost | Usually more affordable | It tends to be more costly |
| 5 | Applications | Filtration, screen printing | Heavy-duty tasks, fencing, concrete |

## **Production Process of Woven Mesh Vs Welded Wire Mesh**

Woven mesh and welded wire mesh are two different kinds of mesh structures used for various purposes. They're made differently, and each type has its own characteristics that make it useful for different things.

#### **Woven Mesh**

* Made by weaving together cross wires and line wires, creating a flexible material.
* Mostly made using machines, which makes it faster to produce.
* Good for filtration, analyzing particles, and screen printing.
* Has a set opening size that can be changed by adjusting how it's woven.

#### **Welded Wire Mesh**

* Created by welding parallel longitudinal wires with cross wires at the intersections.
* This process needs more manual work, like threading wire and cutting excess material.
* Great for strong mesh openings, fencing, protective cages, and concrete wire mesh sheets.
* Sturdier because of the welded intersections, keeping their shape even under pressure.

**Note:** Usually Welded Wire Mesh is more expensive than woven wire mesh because it needs more labour to make.

### **Characteristics of Woven Mesh Vs Welded Wire Mesh**

When comparing woven mesh and welded wire mesh, several factors need consideration, including strength, durability, and cost.

* **Strength and Rigidity:** The welded mesh is often considered more rigid and durable than woven wire mesh. It's well-suited for heavy-duty uses like perimeter fencing, protective caging, and reinforcing concrete due to its sturdy nature.
* **Durability:** The welding process gives welded mesh greater durability compared to woven wire mesh. This durability is beneficial for applications that demand higher strength and rigidity.
* **Cost Comparison:** Usually, woven wire mesh is less expensive than welded mesh, making it a more budget-friendly option for applications where extreme mesh strength isn't the main concern.

### **Woven Mesh Vs Welded Wire Mesh - Applications**

Woven wire mesh and welded wire mesh are used in many industries. Woven wire mesh is handy for filtering, making laminates, sieving, [Stirrups](https://www.jindalpanther.com/stirrups.html), [Footings](https://www.jindalpanther.com/footings.html), building structures, and adding strength. It's flexible and useful for filtering things, studying particles, designing buildings, printing screens, security work, and [building](https://www.jindalpanther.com/building-guide).

On the other hand, welded mesh is great for strengthening concrete, security work, farming, industry needs, and homes. Both kinds of wire mesh are tough, flexible, and can be used in lots of different industries.

![](https://docs.jindalpanther.com/jindalpanther/custom/1719593736CostDifferencesbetweenWovenMeshVsWeldedMesh.webp)

### **Cost Differences between Woven Mesh Vs Welded Mesh**

Jindal Panther offers two types of steel products for construction: Woven Wire Mesh and Welded Wire Mesh. The cost difference between them comes from how they're made and the materials used. Here's why they vary in price:

| Sr. No. | Factors | Woven Wire Mesh | Welded Wire Mesh |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | Production Process | Automated weaving (lower labour cost) | More manual labour (higher labour cost) |
| 2 | Materials | Thinner wires (lower material cost) | Thicker wires (higher material cost) |
| 3 | Labour Requirements | Less labour-intensive (lower cost) | More labour-intensive (higher cost) |
| 4 | Overall Price | Lower | Higher |

### **Common Industries that use Welded Wire Mesh**

Welded wire mesh is really strong and can be used in many different industries because of that. Here are some places where you might find it:

1. **Construction:** In building structures, it helps with filtering, separating stuff, and adding strength.
2. **Agriculture:** Farmers use it for erecting fences, keeping animals safe, and protecting machines.
3. **Industrial:** Big industries use it for fencing purpose, keeping people safe, and storing items.
4. **Transportation:** In cars and trucks, it's used to make frames, cover engines, and protect cargo.
5. **Gardening:** People use it for garden fencing, supporting plants, and related purposes.
6. **Food and Drink:** In the production of food and drinks, it is used for filtering and separation processes.
7. **Pharmaceuticals:** In medicine production, it's used for separation of useful and harmful particles.
8. **Mining:** In mines, it's used for filtering, separating, and making tunnels strong.
9. **Water Treatment:** For cleaning water, it's used for filtering and separation processes.

**Industries That Commonly Use Woven Mesh**

Woven wire mesh is a very handy material used in many different industries. Some of these industries include pharmaceuticals, mining, water treatment, food and drinks, gas and oil, farming, architecture, and big factories. It's used for the following purposes:

* Filtering and separating articles
* Making guards for machines
* Helping with ventilation
* Creating walls and lockers
* Covering surfaces in buildings
* Making things sturdy like handrails, Stirrups, and footings
* Transporting stuff safely
* Keeping things organized on trays
* Cleaning things in special machines
* Making plastic in a certain way
* Helping with chemical and oil filtering

### **Conclusion**

Hence, in picking between woven and welded wire meshes, Jindal Panther the best [TMT rebar distributor](https://www.jindalpanther.com/our-dealer-distributor.html), can help you find what suits you best. These two types have differences. Woven mesh is flexible and cheaper, good for filtering and printing. Welded mesh is tougher, and better for heavy jobs like fencing.

Your choice depends on what you need, strength, flexibility, or cost. This comparison helps you choose wisely. Whether you go for the bendable woven mesh or the strong welded one, pick what fits your project best.

Click here to contact us today for transparent pricing and exceptional quality.

### **Woven Mesh Vs Welded Wire Mesh FAQs**

**Q. What's the main difference between woven mesh and welded wire mesh?**

**Ans:** Woven mesh is made by weaving wires together, while welded wire mesh is created by welding wires at intersections.

**Q. Which is stronger, woven mesh, or welded wire mesh?**

**Ans:** Welded wire mesh is generally considered stronger due to its welded intersections.

**Q. For what purposes are woven mesh and welded wire mesh commonly used?**

**Ans:** Woven mesh finds uses in filtration, architecture, and screen printing, while welded wire mesh is used for heavy-duty applications like fencing and concrete reinforcement.

**Q. Which is more flexible woven mesh, or welded wire mesh?**

**Ans:** Woven mesh tends to be more pliable and flexible compared to welded wire mesh.

**Q. Which is more cost-effective woven mesh, or welded wire mesh?**

**Ans:** Woven mesh is often more cost-effective as it typically involves less labor in its production compared to welded wire mesh.

# Privacy Policy

We care about your data and privacy

Thank you for visiting the website of Jindal Panther, a brand of Jindal Steel. This Privacy Policy explains how Jindal Steel (hereinafter referred to as “Jindal Steel” or “We” or “Our” or “Us”) may collect, use, store, disclose or otherwise process your personal data including personal data provided when using our websites (Jindal Steel websites) and describes the rights you have with respect to your personal data.

Jindal Steel is committed to undertaking reasonable security measures to protect the processing of its employees’, vendors’ and clients’/customers’ personal data based on applicable data protection laws and regulations.

Personal data comprises all the details that Jindal Steel collects and processes directly or indirectly about you as an individual, for instance information about your identity and contact details (such as name, email-ID, contact number), through use of Jindal Steel websites, cookies or other similar tools.

Jindal Steel will not use your personal information for any purpose, other than those described in this statement, without having obtained your express consent.

We may disclose your information to respond to legal requirements, enforce our policies, respond to claims that a listing or other content violates the rights of others, or protect anyone's rights, property, or safety. Such information will be disclosed in accordance with the applicable laws and regulations.

We may also share your information with law enforcement, governmental agencies, or authorized third-parties, in response to a verified request relating to a criminal investigation or alleged illegal activity or any other activity that may expose us, you, or any other stakeholder of the company to legal liability.

We may also share your information with other business entities, should we undergo any corporate restructuring including any plan to merge with or be acquired by that business entity or start a joint venture. (Should such a combination occur, we will require that the new combined entity follow this Privacy Policy with respect to your information).

**Retention of Personal Information**

Your personal data processed by Jindal Steel are kept in a form which permits your identification for no longer than is necessary for the purposes for which the personal data are processed in line with legal, regulatory or statutory obligations. At the expiry of such periods, your personal data will be deleted or archived to comply with legal retention obligations or in accordance with applicable statutory limitation periods.

**Security**

The security and confidentiality of your Personal Data is important to us and Jindal Steel has invested significant resources to protect the safekeeping and confidentiality of your personal data. When using external service providers acting as processors, we require that they adhere to the same standards as Jindal Steel. Regardless of where your personal information is transferred or stored, we take all steps reasonably necessary to ensure that personal data is kept secure.

**Cookies Policy**

Jindal Steel may store some information such as cookies on your computer or device when you visit our website. Cookies are text files containing small amount of information which are downloaded to your device and used to improve your experience of using the Website. You may encounter the cookies from third parties on our website which assist us in certain aspects of our website operations. We do not have control over the content and functionality of these cookies. Please refer to the respective privacy policies of these third parties

You can choose to deny consent to the placing of cookies; however certain features of the Websites may not function fully or as-intended. You can choose to enable or disable Cookies in your internet browser. By default, most internet browsers accept Cookies, but this can be changed. For further details, please consult the help menu in your internet browser.

We use two types of cookies: persistent cookies and session cookies. A persistent cookie lasts beyond the current session and remain at your device until you erase them or they expire. Since a persistent cookie stays in your browser, it will be read by us when you return to our website. Session cookies last only as long as the session (usually the current visit to a website or a browser session). We protect those cookies in a manner that those can only be interpreted for intended purpose.

**Changes to this Privacy Policy**

Jindal Steel may in its absolute discretion update this policy from time to time. We encourage users to periodically check this privacy policy to be updated about the latest policy statements.

The use of the Jindal Steel Websites and any products and services supplied are subject to our Terms and Conditions.

Questions, comments and requests regarding this Privacy Policy should be addressed to info[at]jindalpanther[dot]com

#### STIRRUPS

Manufactured in the most advanced facility. with Italian Technology and sophisticated machinery, Jindal Panther Stirrups maintains the strictest quality standards with consistent 135° angle of rigidity. Jindal Panther Stirrups provides extra strength in Beams and Pillars in all the constructions.

![STIRRUPS]( https://docs.jindalpanther.com/document/123/page/1695289560striups2.png)

[HOME](/) /  TMT Rebar

# TMT Rebar

Jindal Panther TMT Bars banner – high-strength rebars for safe construction

[![Shop Online](https://docs.jindalpanther.com/document/123/custom/1709192296ShopOnline.png)](https://shop.jindalpanther.com/) 

[Overview](#overview) [Advantages](#advantages)  [Product Specifications](#product-specifications) [Gallery](#gallery)

![Send Enquiry](https://docs.jindalpanther.com/static/pantherv2/dist/images/frame-09.png) Send Enquiry

#### TMT Rebars are integral to every construction project.

Jindal Panther TMT Rebars go above and beyond, utilising advanced manufacturing technologies to deliver superior quality rebars that exceed industry standards and elevate the performance of your construction projects.

1. Technologically Advanced Manufacturing: HYQST Technology (MORGAN, USA) and QST Technology (SMS MEER, Germany)
2. Superior Strength: Surpassing the requirements of Fe500D, Fe550D, and Fe600, CRS rebars standards
3. Reliable and Trustworthy: Adhere to stringent quality control measures, ensuring reliable and consistent quality
4. Complement Concrete: Provide the necessary tensile strength required for Reinforced Cement Concrete (RCC) structure

![TMT-Rebars](https://docs.jindalpanther.com/static/pantherv2/dist/images/tmt-rebars-img1.jpg)

## Advantages

![Economical](https://docs.jindalpanther.com/static/pantherv2/dist/images/icon-1.png)

### Economical

Achieve cost-efficient construction with Jindal Panther's TMT Rebars of superior strength, reducing steel consumption while maintaining unmatched quality

![weldability](https://docs.jindalpanther.com/static/pantherv2/dist/images/tmt-icon22.svg)

### Superior weldability

Jindal Panther TMT rebars have low carbon content, making welding safer and faster without requiring preheating. Our HYQST/QST process ensures consistent tensile strength and higher elongation for every inch

![Precision Engineered](https://docs.jindalpanther.com/static/pantherv2/dist/images/tmt-icon33.svg)

### Precision engineered

High-quality steel made from virgin iron ore using advanced refining processes with precise chemical control

![Transparent Pricing](https://docs.jindalpanther.com/static/pantherv2/dist/images/tmt-icon44.svg)

### Transparent pricing model

Our recommended consumer price for Jindal Panther ensures fair pricing, uniformity and transparency for customers around the globe

![High ductility](https://docs.jindalpanther.com/static/pantherv2/dist/images/tmt-icon55.svg)

### High ductility

Crafted using HYQST technology and iron ore, Jindal Panther TMT Rebars have low sulphur and phosphorus content, resulting in superior strength and durability

![Strong Network](https://docs.jindalpanther.com/static/pantherv2/dist/images/tmt-icon66.svg)

### Strong Network

Jindal Panther's extensive network of dealers and distributors, spanning across India, along with its multi-channel retail presence, enables the company to provide unparalleled services to its customers

## Product Specifications

**Diameter of Rebar**

6mm - 50 mm

**Length**

6mm - 12mm  
Straight & Coil Form

**Grades**

Fe500D  
Fe550D  
Fe600  
Fe550D CRS  
Fe500D CRS

Hello There!

[HOME](/) /  Welded Wiremesh

# Welded Wiremesh

Jindal Panther Welded Wire Mesh – durable pre-fabricated grid

[![Shop Online](https://docs.jindalpanther.com/document/123/custom/1709192296ShopOnline.png)](https://shop.jindalpanther.com/) 

[Overview](#overview) [Advantages](#advantages)  [Product Specifications](#product-specifications) [Gallery](#gallery)

[![Send Enquiry](https://docs.jindalpanther.com/static/pantherv2/dist/images/frame-09.png) Send Enquiry](/corporate-office)

#### Welded Wire Mesh

Welded Wire Mesh is an innovative solution from Jindal Panther designed to expedite the construction process. This advanced product consists of rebars welded together, forming a grid pattern that offers exceptional efficiency and convenience.

By utilizing Welded Wire Mesh, construction time is significantly reduced as it eliminates the need for labor-intensive activities such as cutting, marking, spacing of bars, and wire binding. This practical and functional solution provides an ideal and convenient alternative, offering practical advantages and streamlining construction workflows.

![Welded Wire Mesh]( https://docs.jindalpanther.com/static/pantherv2/dist/images/welded-wire-mesh-3.jpg )

## Advantages

![Time Efficiency](https://docs.jindalpanther.com/document/123/custom/1696593216Cost-and-time-efficiency.png)

### Cost and time efficiency

Cost, labor, and time savings that deliver positive results

![Waste reduction](https://docs.jindalpanther.com/document/123/custom/1696593216Waste-reduction-and-optimization.png)

### Waste reduction and optimization

Drastically reduced wastage and scrap, maximizing resource utilization

![Enhanced Accuracy](https://docs.jindalpanther.com/document/123/custom/1696593216Enhanced-accuracy.png)

### Enhanced accuracy

Achieve greater precision and accuracy while optimizing manpower requirements

![Improved productivity](https://docs.jindalpanther.com/document/123/custom/1696593216Improved-productivity.png)

### Improved productivity

Increased output through precise steel area and spacing

![Durable structures](https://docs.jindalpanther.com/document/123/custom/1696593216Durable-structures.png)

### Durable structures

Enhanced structural integrity, ensuring stress transfer and minimizing cracks

![Precision engineered](https://docs.jindalpanther.com/document/123/custom/1696593216Precision-engineered.png)

### Precision engineered

Stronger bonding between rebars facilitated by precise welding techniques

## Product Specifications

**Diameter of bar used**

6mm - 12mm

**Aperture**

50mm - 200mm

**Width**

From 1200mm up to 3200mm  
(max 2500mm with 4mm, 3000mm with 5mm)

**Length**

From 2000mm up to 6000mm
